Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The segmentation variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 226 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 436 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to segmentation theory, we decompose the problem into 226 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 436. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
